209. athāparanti atha idāni itau parampi| pañ‍ca rajānīti rūparāgādīni pañ‍ca rajāni| yaisaṃ satīmā vinayāya sikkhaiti yaisaṃ upaṭṭhitassati hutvāva vinayanatthaṃ tissau sikkhā sikkhaiyya| aivaṃ sikkhantau hi rūpaisu…pai॰… phassaisu sahaitha rāgaṃ, na añ‍ñaiti|

rūparajauti  rūpārammaṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nau rāgādirajau| saddarajādīsupi aisaiva nayau|

210. tatau sau taisaṃ vinayāya sikkhantau anuk‍kamaina – aitaisu dhammaisūti gāthā| tattha aitaisūti rūpādīsu| kālaina sau sammā dhammaṃ parivīmaṃsamānauti sau bhikkhu yvāyaṃ ‘‘uddhatai cittai samathassa  kālau’’tiādinā (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.234) nayaina kālau vuttau, taina kālaina sabbaṃ saṅkhatadhammaṃ anic‍cādinayaina parivīmaṃsamānau| aikaudibhūtau vihanai tamaṃ sauti sau aikaggacittau sabbaṃ mauhāditamaṃ vihanaiyya, natthi aittha saṃsayau|

uddhatai cittaiti vīriyindriyavasaina cittai avūpasantai| balavavīriyañhi mandasamādhiṃ vīriyassa uddhac‍capakkhattā uddhac‍caṃ abhibhavati| aivaṃ uddhatai cittai| samathassa kālauti samādhissa bhāvanāya kālau| samāhitai cittaiti upacārappanāhi cittai samāhitai| balavasamādhi hi mandavīriyaṃ samādhissa kausaj‍japakkhattā kausaj‍jaṃ abhibhavati| samādhi vīriyaina sañ‍ñaujitau kausaj‍jai patituṃ na labhati| vīriyaṃ samādhinā sañ‍ñaujitaṃ uddhac‍cai patituṃ na labhati| tasmā tadubhayaṃ samaṃ kātabbaṃ| ubhayasamatāya hi appanā hauti| vipassanāya kālauti aivaṃ samāhitai anic‍cādivasaina vividhāya passanāya kālau, samādhikammikassa balavatīpi saddhā vaṭṭati| aivaṃ saddahantau aukappaintau appanaṃ pāpuṇāti| samādhipañ‍ñāsu pana samādhikammikassa balavatī aikaggatā vaṭṭati| aivañhi sau appanaṃ pāpuṇāti| vipassanākammikassa pana pañ‍ñā balavatī vaṭṭati| aivañhi sau lakkhaṇappaṭivaidhaṃ pāpuṇāti| ubhin‍naṃ pana samattaipi appanā hauti aiva|

kālai paggaṇhati cittanti yasmiṃ samayai atisithilavīriyatādīhi līnaṃ cittaṃ hauti, tasmiṃ samayai dhammavicayavīriyapītisambaujjhaṅgasamuṭṭhāpanaina taṃ cittaṃ paggaṇhāti| niggaṇhatīti yasmiṃ samayai ac‍cāraddhavīriyatādīhi uddhataṃ cittaṃ hauti, tasmiṃ samayai passaddhisamādhiupaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgasamuṭṭhāpanaina taṃ cittaṃ niggaṇhāti| sampahaṃsati kālaināti yasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ pañ‍ñāpayaugamandatāya vā upasamasukhānaṃ vigamaina vā nirassādaṃ hauti, tasmiṃ samayai aṭṭhasaṃvaigavatthupac‍cavaikkhaṇaina saṃvaijaiti| aṭṭha saṃvaigavatthūni nāma – jātijarābyādhimaraṇāni cattāri, apāyadukkhaṃ pañ‍camaṃ, atītai vaṭṭamūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ anāgatai vaṭṭamūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ, pac‍cuppan‍nai āhārapariyaiṭṭhimūlakaṃ dukkhanti| ratanattayaguṇānussaraṇaināssa pasādaṃ janaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘sampahaṃsati kālainā’’ti| kālai cittaṃ samādahaiti yasmiṃ samayai  saddhāpañ‍ñānaṃ samādhivīriyānañ‍ca samabhāvau, tasmiṃ kālai cittaṃ samādahaiyya|

ajjhupaikkhati kālaināti yasmiṃ samayai sammā paṭipattiṃ āgamma alīnaṃ anuddhataṃ anirassādaṃ ārammaṇai samappavattaṃ samathavīthipaṭipan‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti, tadāssa paggahaniggahasampahaṃsanaisu na byāpāraṃ āpaj‍jati sārathi viya samappavattaisu assaisu| ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘ajjhupaikkhati kālainā’’ti| sau yaugī kālakauvidauti aisau vuttappakārau kammaṭṭhānayaugai niyuttau paggahaniggahasampahaṃsanasamādahanaajjhupaikkhanakālaisu chaikau byattau| kimhi kālamhītiādinā paggahādikālaṃ pucchati|

idāni paggahādikālaṃ vissaj‍jaintau ‘‘līnai cittamhī’’tiādimāha| atisithilavīriyatādīhi cittai līnabhāvaṃ gatai dhammavicayavīriyapītisambaujjhaṅgasamuṭṭhāpanaina paggahau| uddhatasmiṃ viniggahauti ac‍cāraddhavīriyatādīhi uddhatai cittai passaddhisamādhiupaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgasamuṭṭhāpanaina niggahau| nirassādagataṃ cittaṃ, sampahaṃsaiyya tāvadaiti pañ‍ñāpayaugamandatāya vā upasamasukhānaṃ  vigamaina vā assādavirahitaṃ gataṃ aṭṭhasaṃvaigavatthupac‍cavaikkhaṇaina vā ratanattayaguṇānussaraṇaina vā tasmiṃ khaṇai cittaṃ sampahaṃsaiyya|

sampahaṭṭhaṃ yadā cittanti yasmiṃ kālai vuttanayainaiva sampahaṃsitaṃ cittaṃ hauti| alīnaṃ bhavati nuddhatanti vīriyasamādhīhi sañ‍ñaujitattā līnuddhac‍cavirahitañ‍ca hauti| samathanimittassāti samathau ca nimittañ‍ca samathanimittaṃ, tassa samathanimittassa| sau kālauti yau sau līnuddhac‍cavirahitakālau vuttau, sau kālau| ajjhattaṃ ramayai manauti jhānasampayuttaṃ cittaṃ kasiṇādigaucarajjhattai tausaiyya abhiramāpaiyya|

aitaina  maivupāyaināti aitaina vuttaupāyaina aiva| ma-kārau padasandhivasaina vuttau| ajjhupaikkhaiyya tāvadaiti yadā upacārappanāhi taṃ cittaṃ samāhitaṃ, tadā ‘‘samāhitaṃ citta’’nti jānitvā paggahaniggahasampahaṃsanaisu byāpāraṃ akatvā tasmiṃ khaṇai ajjhupaikkhanamaiva karaiyya|

idāni  ‘‘kimhi kālamhi paggāhau’’ti puṭṭhagāthaṃ nigamaintau ‘‘aivaṃ kālavidū dhīrau’’tiādimāha| kālaina kālaṃ cittassa, nimittamupalakkhayaiti kālānukālaṃ samādhisampayuttacittassa ārammaṇaṃ sal‍lakkhaiyya, upaparikkhaiyyāti atthau| saisaṃ sabbattha pākaṭamaiva| aivaṃ arahattanikūṭaina daisanaṃ niṭṭhāpaisīti|

saddhammappaj‍jautikāya mahāniddaisaṭṭhakathāya

sāriputtasuttaniddaisavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

aṭṭhakavaggavaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

saddhammappaj‍jautikā nāma mahāniddaisa-aṭṭhakathā niṭṭhitā|
